Business tip baked inNever attempt to digitize an ancient marketplace by bypassing its traditional authority structures; respect local governance and build tools that empower the existing leadership.
Relationship insight baked inPublicly defending your partner’s honor and competence in hostile rooms cements a bond that private flattery can never achieve.
Good Strategy / Bad Strategy lesson (Rumelt)Identify the proximate objective. Trying to conquer an entire city’s commerce in one move fails; winning the trust of the central market guild opens every door.
S02 · E04
The Ghost in the Commit

Business tip baked inNever operate high-density server infrastructure without redundant physical mechanical overrides on automated fire suppression airlocks.
Relationship insight baked inFacing the immediate mortality of someone you love strips away every polite lie and professional boundary you've erected.
Good Strategy / Bad Strategy lesson (Rumelt)A chain is only as strong as its weakest link. Bad strategy builds multi-million dollar software on top of vulnerable single-point physical hardware.

Business tip baked inCounter predatory copycat apps not with price cuts, but by anchoring your platform to physical infrastructure — like verified pickup lockers and merchant relationship networks — that digital clones cannot replicate.

Good Strategy / Bad Strategy lesson (Rumelt)Create asymmetrical advantage. When a rival uses political influence to attack your distribution, counterattack through unassailable merchant loyalty.

Severing the Swiss routing node. Injecting multi-sig lock: Adaeze Obi, Kwame Asante, David Mensah. Zero unauthorized withdrawals permitted.
SHOT 21 (2:40 – 2:48)
The countdown hits 00:03; the screen flashes emerald green: 'TRANSFER BLOCKED. ASSETS REVERTED TO ACCRA ESCROW.'
Kwame
(exhaling in disbelief, slapping the table)
Transfer aborted! The thirty-five million is back in the treasury!
SHOT 22 (2:48 – 2:56) | CLIFF
The room falls quiet. Across the screen, an incoming encrypted message overrides the terminal: 'WELL PLAYED, ADAEZE. TOBI SAVED HIS CODE, BUT HE CANNOT SAVE YOU. MEET ME IN CANTONMENTS — OR WATCH OFIA BURN.'
Ada
(in her heart, staring at the screen with cold steel resolve)
The Ghost Investor has finally shown his hand. He thinks he can break us. But he has no idea what happens when a market woman goes to war.
Business tip baked inDeploy multi-signature time-locked escrow controls on all treasury clearing accounts so that no single executive key can authorize cross-border settlements.
Relationship insight baked inTrue love demands uncomfortable honesty; withholding critical truths to 'protect' your partner is an act of fear, not love.
Good Strategy / Bad Strategy lesson (Rumelt)Action without coherent diagnosis is futile. Facing the brutal facts of your internal compromise is the prerequisite for any turnaround.

AI Production Bible

Reference prompts, cinematic 16:9 widescreen framing guidelines, and voice notes for generating this series with AI video/image tools, so every character, location, and camera angle stays consistent across fifty seasons and multiple production batches.

Cinematic 16:9 Widescreen Standard & Camera Language

Non-negotiable visual composition rules for all AI video and image generations.

  • 16:9 Widescreen Aspect Ratio (Horizontal): All shots across all episodes must be rendered in 16:9 widescreen (1.78:1 / anamorphic 2.39:1 scope emulation). Vertical (9:16) format is strictly deprecated. Widescreen provides the canvas for authentic West African environmental storytelling — from the bustling market corridors of Aba and Accra to the sweeping glass architecture of Victoria Island.
  • Lens Language & Optics: 35mm and 50mm anamorphic prime lens emulation, wide aperture (f/1.8 – f/2.8) yielding shallow depth of field, creamy bokeh, subtle horizontal anamorphic lens flares on practical lights, and natural filmic grain.
  • Horizontal Staging & Two-Shots: Take advantage of the 16:9 frame for multi-character blocking. Romantic beats between Ada and Tobi must use intimate horizontal two-shots capturing eye-lines and physical proximity. Boardroom showdowns must utilize deep-focus wide compositions that establish spatial power dynamics.
  • Color Palette & Lighting: High-contrast cinematic grade. Warm amber and golden tungsten practicals in Aba; cool teal, slate gray, and corporate signal-blue in Ofia Lagos HQ; vibrant tropical sunlight and terracotta warmth in Accra.

How each principal should sound, regardless of which AI voice model renders them.

Quality Checklist

Run every episode against this before it ships.

  • Does the audience have a reason to watch the next episode?
  • Is there an unanswered question at the end?
  • Is every shot framed and designed for cinematic 16:9 widescreen composition (with horizontal environmental depth and multi-character two-shots)?
  • Does the episode contain between 3 and 7 distinct scenes with proper location/time sluglines?
  • Is romance or romantic tension organically woven into this episode (mandatory every episode)?
  • Does the episode respect the stakes cadence (physical life-or-death capped at 1 in 4 episodes; grounded commercial/relational stakes elsewhere)?
  • Does every single shot carry spoken dialogue or a character's internal heart-thought (zero silent shots)?
  • Does this episode feature a dramatized flashback event if it is part of the 4-episode flashback cadence?
  • Is exactly one business tip demonstrated through action, not narration?
  • Is exactly one relationship insight earned through a choice a character makes on-screen?
  • Is exactly one Good Strategy / Bad Strategy lesson dramatized through a decision, not stated as an aphorism?
  • Does an Ofia product solve a real problem a character has in this episode — without anyone saying an ad line?
  • Would the audience post about this episode online?

Scaling Rules

How to maintain quality as the series grows from 24 episodes to 1,200+.

  • One new mystery per era. Don't open a new long-running mystery until the previous era's mystery is at its midpoint resolution.
  • Maximum 3 active romance arcs at once. Ada & Tobi, Bubbles & Chidi, and one new arc per era — no more, or none of them land.
  • Return characters before audiences forget them. Anyone absent more than two seasons gets a reintroduction beat.
  • Expand countries in pairs. Each new market introduces one new character who connects to an existing one — the world grows through relationships, not geography.
  • Episode 1 of every season must work as an entry point. A first-time viewer must understand who Ada is and why they should care by minute two.
  • Retire characters with purpose. Any departure (promotion, resignation, death, relocation) must visibly affect at least three other characters.
  • The mystery resolution must earn the build-up. A ten-season mystery needs a revelation that recontextualizes something the audience already saw — never just new information.
  • Never break the ad-free promise of the fiction. A product only appears when it is the actual solution to the scene's actual problem. If it wouldn't hold up as real Ofia documentation, it doesn't go in the episode.
  • Scene structure, 16:9 widescreen framing, romance & stakes cadence. Every episode contains 3–7 scenes, is rendered in 16:9 cinematic widescreen, weaves in an organic romantic beat, enforces 100% dialogue/heart-thought coverage, limits physical life/death peril to 1 in 4 episodes, and dramatizes flashback events every four episodes.
  • The Content Bible is a living document. Update the Character Bible, Relationship Map, and Master Timeline after every episode.

Product-integration rule (the sales mandate)

How the show "sells Ofia to the world" without ever feeling like an ad.

  • Problem first, product second. Establish the character's real pain (can't track stock, can't find customers, can't get paid safely) before the product appears.
  • Show the fix, don't say the feature name in dialogue more than once per episode. Let the interface, the outcome, or a support call (Bubbles) carry the brand.
  • Rotate the product spotlight. Across a season, every module — Compass, Shop, Office, ERP, AI Assistant, Business Hub, Logistics, Follow — gets at least one real moment.
  • Tie the product to the mystery, not just the merchant-of-the-week. The Merchant Protection Fund is both a real Ofia feature and the engine of the entire first mystery.
